    def test_the_refusal_reason_reaches_the_operator_verbatim(tmp_path):
        """The provider's own words reach the position AND the operator.
    
        The position carries the CLASSIFIED refusal (it stays unresolved because no
        valid executable SELL quote was obtained for the acquired quantity); the
        message carries the CLASSIFIED refusal plus the provider's own detail, so the
        operator never has to guess between a throttled venue and a dead route.

tmp_path = PosixPath('/tmp/pytest-of-root/pytest-158/test_the_ledger_record_precede0')

    def test_the_ledger_record_precedes_the_close_receipt(self, tmp_path):
        """
        persist -> commit -> emit, asserted by ORDER.
    
        The file is read at the instant each receipt is delivered. If the write
        happened after the notification -- or never -- this sees an empty
        ledger and fails. This is the check that separates "the trade closed"
        from "the closed trade is durable".
        """
        trader = _trader(tmp_path)

______ TestNewestFirst.test_a_new_close_tops_the_ledger_over_old_history _______

self = <tests.test_closed_position_ledger.TestNewestFirst object at 0x7fb6fc9265d0>
tmp_path = PosixPath('/tmp/pytest-of-root/pytest-158/test_a_new_close_tops_the_ledg0')

    def test_a_new_close_tops_the_ledger_over_old_history(self, tmp_path):
        """
        The exact shape of the incident: a ledger full of 2026-10-02 history
        that must not move, with the new settlement at the top.
        """
        for i in range(3):
            _seed_legacy_exit(tmp_path, trade_id=f"old_{i}", symbol=f"OLD{i}",

    def test_trades_and_arm_exits_stay_separate_accounting_bases(self, tmp_path):
        """
        They are SHOWN together and summed by nobody. ``trades()`` must keep
        returning only legacy EXIT records, or the statistics start mixing a
        mark-to-mark book with a fill-vs-fill book.
    
        This used to assert ``len(arm_exits()) > 1``, which asserted the arm
        COUNT rather than the separation -- and the arm count was three only
        because an unrestored strategy selection used to fan out to every

        directly: every arm exit is an arm exit, and none of them appears in the
        legacy book.

tests/test_effective_entry_gate.py:293: 
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 
tests/test_effective_entry_gate.py:179: in beta_engine
    arms=parse_arms(arms if arms is not None else [BETA_GROUP]),
         ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

names = ['V2_FLOOR_BETA']

    def parse_arms(names: Sequence[str]) -> tuple[ArmSpec, ...]:
        """
        Resolve a comma-separated arm list.
    
        An EMPTY list means EVERY SELECTABLE arm, and that is a DELIBERATE,
        EXPLICIT meaning rather than a default: ``parse_arms([])`` is how the
        standalone multi-arm research harness asks for all of them.
    
        "Every" is the SELECTABLE set, and it must be exactly what
        :func:`arm_names` returns -- the same vocabulary the picker and the config
        layer read. It used to be every REGISTERED arm, which silently included a
        retired one and made the two disagree about how many arms exist.
    
        It is NOT what an unselected runtime resolves to. That is
        :data:`DEFAULT_ARM`, resolved by ``RuntimeState.arms``, because "run
        everything" and "the operator chose nothing" are different situations and
        conflating them is what produced the ``L25_TP100`` selection defect.
        A RETIRED arm is refused rather than resolved: an arm that can no longer be
        selected must not be startable through the back door of a persisted list or
        a config file.
    
        ``V2_FLOOR_BETA`` (the group) is NO LONGER ACTIVE. The V2 FLOOR (BETA)
        experiment has been disabled from the active bot. Any selection that names
        the group token or a beta arm token (V2_BETA_A, V2_BETA_B, V2_BETA_C) is
        REFUSED rather than expanded, so the experiment cannot be started through
        the selector, a persisted list, or a config file.
    
        A token that names a BETA ARM (``V2_BETA_A``) previously resolved to the
        SAME group, not to that arm alone. Running one arm of a three-arm comparison
        is a different experiment and must never be mistaken for it, so a partial
        group is not a reachable outcome.
    
        The beta arm specs, enum values, and historical records are preserved: they
        remain resolvable by :func:`arm_by_name` for diagnostics and existing
        position records, but they are NOT offered for new selection and the group
        token is refused.
        """
        wanted = [n.strip() for n in names if str(n).strip()]
        if not wanted:
            # Empty means every SELECTABLE arm -- and the beta arms are NOT
            # selectable on their own, so an unselected configuration cannot
            # accidentally start the V2 FLOOR (BETA) experiment. The Amarih arms
            # are likewise group-only, so an empty selection never starts Amarih
            # either: it resolves to the L25-family production arms.
            return tuple(ARMS[a] for a in selectable_arms())
        out: list[ArmSpec] = []
        for n in wanted:
            if is_beta_group(n) or _is_beta_arm_token(n):
                # V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled. Refuse rather than expand.
>               raise ValueError(
                    f"V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled from the active bot. "
                    f"Selection of {n!r} is no longer accepted. "
                    "The experiment's code and historical records are preserved, "
                    "but no new beta positions can be opened.")
E               ValueError: V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled from the active bot. Selection of 'V2_FLOOR_BETA' is no longer accepted. The experiment's code and historical records are preserved, but no new beta positions can be opened.

tests/test_effective_entry_gate.py:327: AssertionError
_ TestPerStrategyAuthorizationIsIndependent.test_both_directions_at_the_decision_layer _

self = <tests.test_effective_entry_gate.TestPerStrategyAuthorizationIsIndependent object at 0x7fb6fc86ce10>

    def test_both_directions_at_the_decision_layer(self):
        """
        THE INVERSE-GATE MATRIX.
    
        Rows are candidates, columns are selections. The cell is the decision.
        A strategy's refusal must never remove another strategy's authorization.
        """
        matrix = {
            "V2_ok/L25_no": (
                V2_OK_L25_NO,
                {"beta": Decision.BUY, "l25": Decision.REJECT,
                 "both": Decision.BUY}),
            "V2_no/L25_ok": (
                V2_NO_L25_OK,
                {"beta": Decision.REJECT, "l25": Decision.BUY,
                 "both": Decision.BUY}),
            "both_ok": (
                BOTH_OK,
                {"beta": Decision.BUY, "l25": Decision.BUY,
                 "both": Decision.BUY}),
        }

                    selected_arms=selections[key], **features)

tests/test_effective_entry_gate.py:451:

tests/test_effective_entry_gate.py:179: in beta_engine
    arms=parse_arms(arms if arms is not None else [BETA_GROUP]),
         ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

names = ['L25_CURRENT', 'V2_FLOOR_BETA']

    def parse_arms(names: Sequence[str]) -> tuple[ArmSpec, ...]:
        """
        Resolve a comma-separated arm list.
    
        An EMPTY list means EVERY SELECTABLE arm, and that is a DELIBERATE,
        EXPLICIT meaning rather than a default: ``parse_arms([])`` is how the
        standalone multi-arm research harness asks for all of them.
    
        "Every" is the SELECTABLE set, and it must be exactly what
        :func:`arm_names` returns -- the same vocabulary the picker and the config
        layer read. It used to be every REGISTERED arm, which silently included a
        retired one and made the two disagree about how many arms exist.
    
        It is NOT what an unselected runtime resolves to. That is
        :data:`DEFAULT_ARM`, resolved by ``RuntimeState.arms``, because "run
        everything" and "the operator chose nothing" are different situations and
        conflating them is what produced the ``L25_TP100`` selection defect.
        A RETIRED arm is refused rather than resolved: an arm that can no longer be
        selected must not be startable through the back door of a persisted list or
        a config file.
    
        ``V2_FLOOR_BETA`` (the group) is NO LONGER ACTIVE. The V2 FLOOR (BETA)
        experiment has been disabled from the active bot. Any selection that names
        the group token or a beta arm token (V2_BETA_A, V2_BETA_B, V2_BETA_C) is
        REFUSED rather than expanded, so the experiment cannot be started through
        the selector, a persisted list, or a config file.
    
        A token that names a BETA ARM (``V2_BETA_A``) previously resolved to the
        SAME group, not to that arm alone. Running one arm of a three-arm comparison
        is a different experiment and must never be mistaken for it, so a partial
        group is not a reachable outcome.
    
        The beta arm specs, enum values, and historical records are preserved: they
        remain resolvable by :func:`arm_by_name` for diagnostics and existing
        position records, but they are NOT offered for new selection and the group
        token is refused.
        """
        wanted = [n.strip() for n in names if str(n).strip()]
        if not wanted:
            # Empty means every SELECTABLE arm -- and the beta arms are NOT
            # selectable on their own, so an unselected configuration cannot
            # accidentally start the V2 FLOOR (BETA) experiment. The Amarih arms
            # are likewise group-only, so an empty selection never starts Amarih
            # either: it resolves to the L25-family production arms.
            return tuple(ARMS[a] for a in selectable_arms())
        out: list[ArmSpec] = []
        for n in wanted:
            if is_beta_group(n) or _is_beta_arm_token(n):
                # V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled. Refuse rather than expand.
>               raise ValueError(
                    f"V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled from the active bot. "
                    f"Selection of {n!r} is no longer accepted. "
                    "The experiment's code and historical records are preserved, "
                    "but no new beta positions can be opened.")
E               ValueError: V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled from the active bot. Selection of 'V2_FLOOR_BETA' is no longer accepted. The experiment's code and historical records are preserved, but no new beta positions can be opened.

         ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

names = ['V2_FLOOR_BETA']

    def parse_arms(names: Sequence[str]) -> tuple[ArmSpec, ...]:
        """
        Resolve a comma-separated arm list.
    
        An EMPTY list means EVERY SELECTABLE arm, and that is a DELIBERATE,
        EXPLICIT meaning rather than a default: ``parse_arms([])`` is how the
        standalone multi-arm research harness asks for all of them.
    
        "Every" is the SELECTABLE set, and it must be exactly what
        :func:`arm_names` returns -- the same vocabulary the picker and the config
        layer read. It used to be every REGISTERED arm, which silently included a
        retired one and made the two disagree about how many arms exist.
    
        It is NOT what an unselected runtime resolves to. That is
        :data:`DEFAULT_ARM`, resolved by ``RuntimeState.arms``, because "run
        everything" and "the operator chose nothing" are different situations and
        conflating them is what produced the ``L25_TP100`` selection defect.
        A RETIRED arm is refused rather than resolved: an arm that can no longer be
        selected must not be startable through the back door of a persisted list or
        a config file.
    
        ``V2_FLOOR_BETA`` (the group) is NO LONGER ACTIVE. The V2 FLOOR (BETA)
        experiment has been disabled from the active bot. Any selection that names
        the group token or a beta arm token (V2_BETA_A, V2_BETA_B, V2_BETA_C) is
        REFUSED rather than expanded, so the experiment cannot be started through
        the selector, a persisted list, or a config file.
    
        A token that names a BETA ARM (``V2_BETA_A``) previously resolved to the
        SAME group, not to that arm alone. Running one arm of a three-arm comparison
        is a different experiment and must never be mistaken for it, so a partial
        group is not a reachable outcome.
    
        The beta arm specs, enum values, and historical records are preserved: they
        remain resolvable by :func:`arm_by_name` for diagnostics and existing
        position records, but they are NOT offered for new selection and the group
        token is refused.
        """
        wanted = [n.strip() for n in names if str(n).strip()]
        if not wanted:
            # Empty means every SELECTABLE arm -- and the beta arms are NOT
            # selectable on their own, so an unselected configuration cannot
            # accidentally start the V2 FLOOR (BETA) experiment. The Amarih arms
            # are likewise group-only, so an empty selection never starts Amarih
            # either: it resolves to the L25-family production arms.
            return tuple(ARMS[a] for a in selectable_arms())
        out: list[ArmSpec] = []
        for n in wanted:
            if is_beta_group(n) or _is_beta_arm_token(n):
                # V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled. Refuse rather than expand.
>               raise ValueError(
                    f"V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled from the active bot. "
                    f"Selection of {n!r} is no longer accepted. "
                    "The experiment's code and historical records are preserved, "
                    "but no new beta positions can be opened.")
E               ValueError: V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled from the active bot. Selection of 'V2_FLOOR_BETA' is no longer accepted. The experiment's code and historical records are preserved, but no new beta positions can be opened.

    def test_stop_stops_new_entries_but_keeps_managing(config):
        import asyncio
    
        clock = FakeClock(T0)
        venue = Venue(clock)
        venue.add(MINT, [1.0, 1.05, 1.10, 1.15, 1.2, 1.25, 1.3])
        venue.add(MINT2, [1.0, 1.05, 1.1, 1.15, 1.2, 1.25, 1.3])
        trader = make_trader(config, clock, venue)
        trader.startup()
        trader.state.start()   # the operator start button
    
        async def run():
            await trader.on_alert(snap(clock, MINT, "A", venue.price(MINT), 1), _parsed(None))
            await _drive(trader, clock, 3)
            trader.control.command = "STOP"
            await trader.on_alert(snap(clock, MINT2, "B", venue.price(MINT2), 2), _parsed(None))
            assert trader.candidates_seen == 2
            assert all(p.mint != MINT2 for p in trader.account.open_positions())
            # positions are still managed
            await _drive(trader, clock, 6)
            # a stop is not a flatten
            assert len(trader.account.open_positions()) == 1

    def test_market_going_dark_marks_data_uncertain_rather_than_guessing(config):
        import asyncio
    
        clock = FakeClock(T0)
        venue = Venue(clock)
        venue.add(MINT, [1.0, 1.03, 1.06, 1.09, 1.12, 1.15, 1.18, 1.2, 1.22, 1.24,
                         1.26, 1.28, 1.30, 1.32, 1.34, 1.36], base=1.0e-4)
        trader = make_trader(config, clock, venue)
        trader.startup()
        trader.state.start()   # the operator start button
    
        async def run():
            await trader.on_alert(snap(clock, MINT, "D", venue.price(MINT)), _parsed(None))
            await _drive(trader, clock, 2)
            p = trader.account.open_positions()[0]
            venue.fail.add(MINT)
            # enough cycles for the data to go past the unusable threshold
            await _drive(trader, clock, 45)
            assert p.data_state in (DataState.UNUSABLE, DataState.STALE)
            # equity is still coherent, valued at cost rather than a made-up price
            assert trader.account.snapshot()["identity_check"] == pytest.approx(0.0, abs=1e-9)
            # and the position was exited once data had been bad long enough
            assert p.status in (PositionStatus.CLOSED, PositionStatus.OPEN)
    
>       asyncio.new_event_loop().run_until_complete(run())

self = <tests.test_execution_aware_runtime.TestRuntime object at 0x7fb6fc7c08d0>

    def test_the_book_is_loaded_at_startup_not_at_the_first_signal(
            self, tmp_path, monkeypatch):
        """
        THE DEFERRAL DEFECT.
    
        The execution-aware PAPER bridge was built ONLY on the entry path, and
        ``monitor_once`` skips that book while it does not exist::
    
            if self._execution_aware_paper() and self._paper_bridge is not None:
                trades = await self._paper_bridge.tick()
    
        So a restart left the whole book -- open positions AND the V2 FLOOR
        (BETA) arms that are still waiting for an executable SELL quote --
        unmonitored until some unrelated candidate happened to be authorised,
        which under a BETA-only selection the V2 FLOOR gate can refuse for
        hours. In the production run this was found in, the previous process
        retried its three unresolved beta arms every ~6s up to its shutdown and
        the process that replaced it retried NOTHING for the next fifteen
        minutes.
    
        Asserted as BEHAVIOUR, with no signal delivered anywhere: the boot step
        builds the book, loads the experiment that owns those arms, and the very
        next monitor tick reaches it.
        """
        trader = PaperTrader(_config(tmp_path), market=_Market(), nar=_Nar(),
                             quote_service=FakeQuoteService(),
                             sol_usd_provider=_sol_usd_provider())
        # The operator's selection lives on the runtime state; the bridge
        # captures it at construction, which is what this test pins.
        ok, message = trader.state.select_strategies([BETA_GROUP])
>       assert ok, message
E       AssertionError: V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled from the active bot. Selection of 'V2_FLOOR_BETA' is no longer accepted. The experiment's code and historical records are preserved, but no new beta positions can be opened.
E       assert False

    def test_a_refused_entry_records_a_reason_in_the_canonical_outcome(
        self, tmp_path):
        """
        The counterpart defect: a BUY that filled nothing must not keep reading
        ``executed=False`` with ``reason=None``, which is indistinguishable
        from "never got that far".
        """
        trader = _trader(tmp_path)
        bridge = trader._paper_bridge
        bridge.sol_reference = None
        bridge.quote_service.sol_reference = None
        run(trader._execute_paper_buy_execution_aware(
            real_report(), alert(), _features()))
        assert not bridge.open_positions()
        assert trader.last_execution["executed"] is False
        # The outcome names the ACTUAL refusal rather than a vague bucket. It
        # used to read "BUY_BLOCKED" while ``reason`` said something entirely
        # different, so an operator had to know to read the second field; today
        # the two agree, and neither can be None on an attempted entry.

    def _execute_child(self, args, executable, preexec_fn, close_fds,
                       pass_fds, cwd, env,
                       startupinfo, creationflags, shell,
                       p2cread, p2cwrite,
                       c2pread, c2pwrite,
                       errread, errwrite,
                       restore_signals,
                       gid, gids, uid, umask,
                       start_new_session, process_group):
        """Execute program (POSIX version)"""
    
        if isinstance(args, (str, bytes)):
            args = [args]
        elif isinstance(args, os.PathLike):
            if shell:
                raise TypeError('path-like args is not allowed when '
                                'shell is true')
            args = [args]
        else:
            args = list(args)
    
        if shell:
            # On Android the default shell is at '/system/bin/sh'.
            unix_shell = ('/system/bin/sh' if
                      hasattr(sys, 'getandroidapilevel') else '/bin/sh')
            args = [unix_shell, "-c"] + args
            if executable:
                args[0] = executable
    
        if executable is None:
            executable = args[0]
    
        sys.audit("subprocess.Popen", executable, args, cwd, env)
    
        if (_USE_POSIX_SPAWN
                and os.path.dirname(executable)
                and preexec_fn is None
                and (not close_fds or _HAVE_POSIX_SPAWN_CLOSEFROM)
                and not pass_fds
                and cwd is None
                and (p2cread == -1 or p2cread > 2)
                and (c2pwrite == -1 or c2pwrite > 2)
                and (errwrite == -1 or errwrite > 2)
                and not start_new_session
                and process_group == -1
                and gid is None
                and gids is None
                and uid is None
                and umask < 0):
            self._posix_spawn(args, executable, env, restore_signals, close_fds,
                              p2cread, p2cwrite,
                              c2pread, c2pwrite,
                              errread, errwrite)
            return
    
        orig_executable = executable
    
        # For transferring possible exec failure from child to parent.
        # Data format: "exception name:hex errno:description"
        # Pickle is not used; it is complex and involves memory allocation.
        errpipe_read, errpipe_write = os.pipe()
        # errpipe_write must not be in the standard io 0, 1, or 2 fd range.
        low_fds_to_close = []
        while errpipe_write < 3:
            low_fds_to_close.append(errpipe_write)
            errpipe_write = os.dup(errpipe_write)
        for low_fd in low_fds_to_close:
            os.close(low_fd)
        try:
            try:
                # We must avoid complex work that could involve
                # malloc or free in the child process to avoid
                # potential deadlocks, thus we do all this here.
                # and pass it to fork_exec()
    
                if env is not None:
                    env_list = []
                    for k, v in env.items():
                        k = os.fsencode(k)
                        if b'=' in k:
                            raise ValueError("illegal environment variable name")
                        env_list.append(k + b'=' + os.fsencode(v))
                else:
                    env_list = None  # Use execv instead of execve.
                executable = os.fsencode(executable)
                if os.path.dirname(executable):
                    executable_list = (executable,)
                else:
                    # This matches the behavior of os._execvpe().
                    executable_list = tuple(
                        os.path.join(os.fsencode(dir), executable)
                        for dir in os.get_exec_path(env))
                fds_to_keep = set(pass_fds)
                fds_to_keep.add(errpipe_write)
                self.pid = _fork_exec(
                        args, executable_list,
                        close_fds, tuple(sorted(map(int, fds_to_keep))),
                        cwd, env_list,
                        p2cread, p2cwrite, c2pread, c2pwrite,
                        errread, errwrite,
                        errpipe_read, errpipe_write,
                        restore_signals, start_new_session,
                        process_group, gid, gids, uid, umask,
                        preexec_fn, _USE_VFORK)
                self._child_created = True
            finally:
                # be sure the FD is closed no matter what
                os.close(errpipe_write)
    
            self._close_pipe_fds(p2cread, p2cwrite,
                                 c2pread, c2pwrite,
                                 errread, errwrite)
    
            # Wait for exec to fail or succeed; possibly raising an
            # exception (limited in size)
            errpipe_data = bytearray()
            while True:
                part = os.read(errpipe_read, 50000)
                errpipe_data += part
                if not part or len(errpipe_data) > 50000:
                    break
        finally:
            # be sure the FD is closed no matter what
            os.close(errpipe_read)
    
        if errpipe_data:
            try:
                pid, sts = os.waitpid(self.pid, 0)
                if pid == self.pid:
                    self._handle_exitstatus(sts)
                else:
                    self.returncode = sys.maxsize
            except ChildProcessError:
                pass
    
            try:
                exception_name, hex_errno, err_msg = (
                        errpipe_data.split(b':', 2))
                # The encoding here should match the encoding
                # written in by the subprocess implementations
                # like _posixsubprocess
                err_msg = err_msg.decode()
            except ValueError:
                exception_name = b'SubprocessError'
                hex_errno = b'0'
                err_msg = 'Bad exception data from child: {!r}'.format(
                              bytes(errpipe_data))
            child_exception_type = getattr(
                    builtins, exception_name.decode('ascii'),
                    SubprocessError)
            if issubclass(child_exception_type, OSError) and hex_errno:
                errno_num = int(hex_errno, 16)
                if err_msg == "noexec:chdir":
                    err_msg = ""
                    # The error must be from chdir(cwd).
                    err_filename = cwd
                elif err_msg == "noexec":
                    err_msg = ""
                    err_filename = None
                else:
                    err_filename = orig_executable
                if errno_num != 0:
                    err_msg = os.strerror(errno_num)
                if err_filename is not None:
>                   raise child_exception_type(errno_num, err_msg, err_filename)
E                   FileNotFoundError: [Errno 2] No such file or directory: '/Users/lnwper/AI-paper-trade'

tests/test_paper_state_store_path_safety.py:224: 
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

names = ['V2_FLOOR_BETA']

    def parse_arms(names: Sequence[str]) -> tuple[ArmSpec, ...]:
        """
        Resolve a comma-separated arm list.
    
        An EMPTY list means EVERY SELECTABLE arm, and that is a DELIBERATE,
        EXPLICIT meaning rather than a default: ``parse_arms([])`` is how the
        standalone multi-arm research harness asks for all of them.
    
        "Every" is the SELECTABLE set, and it must be exactly what
        :func:`arm_names` returns -- the same vocabulary the picker and the config
        layer read. It used to be every REGISTERED arm, which silently included a
        retired one and made the two disagree about how many arms exist.
    
        It is NOT what an unselected runtime resolves to. That is
        :data:`DEFAULT_ARM`, resolved by ``RuntimeState.arms``, because "run
        everything" and "the operator chose nothing" are different situations and
        conflating them is what produced the ``L25_TP100`` selection defect.
        A RETIRED arm is refused rather than resolved: an arm that can no longer be
        selected must not be startable through the back door of a persisted list or
        a config file.
    
        ``V2_FLOOR_BETA`` (the group) is NO LONGER ACTIVE. The V2 FLOOR (BETA)
        experiment has been disabled from the active bot. Any selection that names
        the group token or a beta arm token (V2_BETA_A, V2_BETA_B, V2_BETA_C) is
        REFUSED rather than expanded, so the experiment cannot be started through
        the selector, a persisted list, or a config file.
    
        A token that names a BETA ARM (``V2_BETA_A``) previously resolved to the
        SAME group, not to that arm alone. Running one arm of a three-arm comparison
        is a different experiment and must never be mistaken for it, so a partial
        group is not a reachable outcome.
    
        The beta arm specs, enum values, and historical records are preserved: they
        remain resolvable by :func:`arm_by_name` for diagnostics and existing
        position records, but they are NOT offered for new selection and the group
        token is refused.
        """
        wanted = [n.strip() for n in names if str(n).strip()]
        if not wanted:
            # Empty means every SELECTABLE arm -- and the beta arms are NOT
            # selectable on their own, so an unselected configuration cannot
            # accidentally start the V2 FLOOR (BETA) experiment. The Amarih arms
            # are likewise group-only, so an empty selection never starts Amarih
            # either: it resolves to the L25-family production arms.
            return tuple(ARMS[a] for a in selectable_arms())
        out: list[ArmSpec] = []
        for n in wanted:
            if is_beta_group(n) or _is_beta_arm_token(n):
                # V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled. Refuse rather than expand.
>               raise ValueError(
                    f"V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled from the active bot. "
                    f"Selection of {n!r} is no longer accepted. "
                    "The experiment's code and historical records are preserved, "
                    "but no new beta positions can be opened.")

    @contextlib.contextmanager
    def map_httpcore_exceptions() -> typing.Iterator[None]:
        global HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP
        if len(HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP) == 0:
            HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP = _load_httpcore_exceptions()
        try:
            yield
        except Exception as exc:
            mapped_exc = None
    
            for from_exc, to_exc in HTTPCORE_EXC_MAP.items():
                if not isinstance(exc, from_exc):
                    continue
                # We want to map to the most specific exception we can find.
                # Eg if `exc` is an `httpcore.ReadTimeout`, we want to map to
                # `httpx.ReadTimeout`, not just `httpx.TimeoutException`.
                if mapped_exc is None or issubclass(to_exc, mapped_exc):
                    mapped_exc = to_exc
    
            if mapped_exc is None:  # pragma: no cover
                raise
    
            message = str(exc)
>           raise mapped_exc(message) from exc

    async def detect_venue(rpc, mint: str) -> tuple:
        """

tmp_path = PosixPath('/tmp/pytest-of-root/pytest-158/test_the_epoch_survives_a_save0')

    def test_the_epoch_survives_a_save_and_reload(tmp_path):
        """
        The epoch must survive a persist cycle, or a restart would silently widen
        the window back to the whole ledger.
        """
        from autopaper.config import build_config
        from autopaper.engine import PaperTrader
    
        t = PaperTrader(build_config(tmp_path))
        try:
            # persist() refuses without ownership, which is the correct guard, so
            # this takes the lock the way the runtime does
            t.startup()
            object.__setattr__(t.account.state, "stats_epoch_started_at", 4242.0)
            t.persist()
        finally:
            t._release_ownership()
    
        # A RESTART: _load_state() runs in startup(), so the reload must go through
        # it exactly as a real restart would.
        again = PaperTrader(build_config(tmp_path))
        try:
            again.startup()
>           assert again.account.state.stats_epoch_started_at == 4242.0

    def test_beta_was_explicitly_selected_not_a_default_or_a_legacy_fallback(
            self):
        """
        A fresh state runs the DEFAULT arm, and an empty request is REFUSED --
        so the beta arms in the incident file cannot have appeared by default,
        by a missing file, or by a legacy configuration value.

tests/test_v2_floor_beta_entry_attribution.py:276: 
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 
tests/test_v2_floor_beta_entry_attribution.py:128: in incident_engine
    arms=parse_arms(arms),
         ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

names = ['V2_FLOOR_BETA']

    def parse_arms(names: Sequence[str]) -> tuple[ArmSpec, ...]:
        """
        Resolve a comma-separated arm list.
    
        An EMPTY list means EVERY SELECTABLE arm, and that is a DELIBERATE,
        EXPLICIT meaning rather than a default: ``parse_arms([])`` is how the
        standalone multi-arm research harness asks for all of them.
    
        "Every" is the SELECTABLE set, and it must be exactly what
        :func:`arm_names` returns -- the same vocabulary the picker and the config
        layer read. It used to be every REGISTERED arm, which silently included a
        retired one and made the two disagree about how many arms exist.
    
        It is NOT what an unselected runtime resolves to. That is
        :data:`DEFAULT_ARM`, resolved by ``RuntimeState.arms``, because "run
        everything" and "the operator chose nothing" are different situations and
        conflating them is what produced the ``L25_TP100`` selection defect.
        A RETIRED arm is refused rather than resolved: an arm that can no longer be
        selected must not be startable through the back door of a persisted list or
        a config file.
    
        ``V2_FLOOR_BETA`` (the group) is NO LONGER ACTIVE. The V2 FLOOR (BETA)
        experiment has been disabled from the active bot. Any selection that names
        the group token or a beta arm token (V2_BETA_A, V2_BETA_B, V2_BETA_C) is
        REFUSED rather than expanded, so the experiment cannot be started through
        the selector, a persisted list, or a config file.
    
        A token that names a BETA ARM (``V2_BETA_A``) previously resolved to the
        SAME group, not to that arm alone. Running one arm of a three-arm comparison
        is a different experiment and must never be mistaken for it, so a partial
        group is not a reachable outcome.
    
        The beta arm specs, enum values, and historical records are preserved: they
        remain resolvable by :func:`arm_by_name` for diagnostics and existing
        position records, but they are NOT offered for new selection and the group
        token is refused.
        """
        wanted = [n.strip() for n in names if str(n).strip()]
        if not wanted:
            # Empty means every SELECTABLE arm -- and the beta arms are NOT
            # selectable on their own, so an unselected configuration cannot
            # accidentally start the V2 FLOOR (BETA) experiment. The Amarih arms
            # are likewise group-only, so an empty selection never starts Amarih
            # either: it resolves to the L25-family production arms.
            return tuple(ARMS[a] for a in selectable_arms())
        out: list[ArmSpec] = []
        for n in wanted:
            if is_beta_group(n) or _is_beta_arm_token(n):
                # V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled. Refuse rather than expand.
>               raise ValueError(
                    f"V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled from the active bot. "
                    f"Selection of {n!r} is no longer accepted. "
                    "The experiment's code and historical records are preserved, "
                    "but no new beta positions can be opened.")
E               ValueError: V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled from the active bot. Selection of 'V2_FLOOR_BETA' is no longer accepted. The experiment's code and historical records are preserved, but no new beta positions can be opened.

    def parse_arms(names: Sequence[str]) -> tuple[ArmSpec, ...]:
        """
        Resolve a comma-separated arm list.
    
        An EMPTY list means EVERY SELECTABLE arm, and that is a DELIBERATE,
        EXPLICIT meaning rather than a default: ``parse_arms([])`` is how the
        standalone multi-arm research harness asks for all of them.

        :data:`DEFAULT_ARM`, resolved by ``RuntimeState.arms``, because "run
        everything" and "the operator chose nothing" are different situations and
        conflating them is what produced the ``L25_TP100`` selection defect.

        a config file.

        experiment has been disabled from the active bot. Any selection that names

        SAME group, not to that arm alone. Running one arm of a three-arm comparison
        is a different experiment and must never be mistaken for it, so a partial
        group is not a reachable outcome.
    
        The beta arm specs, enum values, and historical records are preserved: they
        remain resolvable by :func:`arm_by_name` for diagnostics and existing
        position records, but they are NOT offered for new selection and the group
        token is refused.

            # selectable on their own, so an unselected configuration cannot
            # accidentally start the V2 FLOOR (BETA) experiment. The Amarih arms
            # are likewise group-only, so an empty selection never starts Amarih
            # either: it resolves to the L25-family production arms.
            return tuple(ARMS[a] for a in selectable_arms())

    def test_l25_and_beta_can_be_selected_together(self):
        """
        The architecture does NOT prevent L25 from running alongside beta.
        PAPER is multi-select, and ``toggle_strategy`` MERGES the group into
        the current selection. This is asserted rather than assumed, because
        the alternative -- the two being mutually exclusive -- would change
        what the whole design means.
        """
        state = RuntimeState()
        assert state.toggle_strategy("L25_CURRENT")[0] is True
        ok, msg = state.toggle_strategy(BETA_GROUP)

        everything" and "the operator chose nothing" are different situations and
        conflating them is what produced the ``L25_TP100`` selection defect.
        A RETIRED arm is refused rather than resolved: an arm that can no longer be
        selected must not be startable through the back door of a persisted list or
        a config file.
    
        ``V2_FLOOR_BETA`` (the group) is NO LONGER ACTIVE. The V2 FLOOR (BETA)
        experiment has been disabled from the active bot. Any selection that names
        the group token or a beta arm token (V2_BETA_A, V2_BETA_B, V2_BETA_C) is
        REFUSED rather than expanded, so the experiment cannot be started through
        the selector, a persisted list, or a config file.
    
        A token that names a BETA ARM (``V2_BETA_A``) previously resolved to the
        SAME group, not to that arm alone. Running one arm of a three-arm comparison
        is a different experiment and must never be mistaken for it, so a partial
        group is not a reachable outcome.
    
        The beta arm specs, enum values, and historical records are preserved: they
        remain resolvable by :func:`arm_by_name` for diagnostics and existing
        position records, but they are NOT offered for new selection and the group
        token is refused.
        """
        wanted = [n.strip() for n in names if str(n).strip()]
        if not wanted:
            # Empty means every SELECTABLE arm -- and the beta arms are NOT
            # selectable on their own, so an unselected configuration cannot
            # accidentally start the V2 FLOOR (BETA) experiment. The Amarih arms
            # are likewise group-only, so an empty selection never starts Amarih
            # either: it resolves to the L25-family production arms.
            return tuple(ARMS[a] for a in selectable_arms())
        out: list[ArmSpec] = []
        for n in wanted:

        SAME group, not to that arm alone. Running one arm of a three-arm comparison
        is a different experiment and must never be mistaken for it, so a partial
        group is not a reachable outcome.
    
        The beta arm specs, enum values, and historical records are preserved: they
        remain resolvable by :func:`arm_by_name` for diagnostics and existing
        position records, but they are NOT offered for new selection and the group
        token is refused.

            # selectable on their own, so an unselected configuration cannot

            return tuple(ARMS[a] for a in selectable_arms())

___ test_the_aggregate_shared_liquidity_view_is_separate_and_moves_no_ledger ___

tmp_path = PosixPath('/tmp/pytest-of-root/pytest-158/test_the_aggregate_shared_liqu0')

    def test_the_aggregate_shared_liquidity_view_is_separate_and_moves_no_ledger(
            tmp_path):
        """A/B/C combined demand is reported as a STRESS VIEW, not a result.
    
        The arms are independent ledgers, so their COMBINED demand -- the size the
        pool would actually absorb if all three acted on the same mint at once -- is
        reported separately, is labelled a stress view, and moves no ledger.
        """
        # (a) an arm holding a position is blocked, so it contributes no demand.
        eng = beta_engine(tmp_path / "held", quotes=SizingQuoteService())
        run(eng.on_signal(eligible_signal()))
        exp = eng.beta_experiment
>       before = {a: exp.accounts[a].bankroll_usd for a in "ABC"}
                     ^^^^^^^^^^^^

    def test_open_and_unresolved_beta_positions_are_not_closed_rows(tmp_path):

    def test_every_new_entry_spends_the_whole_deployable_budget(tmp_path):

____________ test_the_all_in_entry_reserves_the_outside_swap_costs _____________

tmp_path = PosixPath('/tmp/pytest-of-root/pytest-158/test_the_all_in_entry_reserves0')

    def test_the_all_in_entry_reserves_the_outside_swap_costs(tmp_path):
        """The venue fee is INSIDE the quoted input; the fees/rent are held BESIDE it.
    
        Two different things, and conflating them was the defect:
    
        * the SWAP fee is already inside the quoted input amount, and the fill model
          records it as zero so it is never charged twice;
        * the Solana base fee, the priority fee and the token-account rent are paid
          OUTSIDE the swap, so they are held back from the deployable notional.
        """
        eng = _all_in_engine(tmp_path)
        run(eng.on_signal(eligible_signal()))
        exp = eng.beta_experiment
>       pid = next(iter(exp.positions))
                        ^^^^^^^^^^^^^
E       AttributeError: 'NoneType' object has no attribute 'positions'

______ test_sizing_is_reported_per_rule_and_legacy_never_reads_as_all_in _______

tmp_path = PosixPath('/tmp/pytest-of-root/pytest-158/test_sizing_is_reported_per_ru0')

    def test_sizing_is_reported_per_rule_and_legacy_never_reads_as_all_in(tmp_path):
        # a LEGACY record -- written before this change, by the removed 10% rule.
        legacy = {"status": "closed", "arm": "A", "net_pnl_usd": 0.30,
                  "net_return_pct": 30.0, "position_size_usd": 1.0,
                  "pnl_basis": "V2_FLOOR_BETA_MODELED_BACKTEST"}
        assert sizing_bucket(legacy) == "legacy"
        # an EXPLICIT-SIZE probe is not all-in either.
        explicit = {**legacy, "sizing_rule": SIZING_EXPLICIT, "sizing_pct": None}
        assert sizing_bucket(explicit) == "explicit"
        all_in = {**legacy, "sizing_rule": SIZING_ALL_IN, "sizing_pct": 100.0}
        assert sizing_bucket(all_in) == "all_in"
        # a record claiming a sub-100 percentage is legacy, not all-in.
        assert sizing_bucket({**legacy, "sizing_pct": 10.0}) == "legacy"
    
        exp = new_exp(tmp_path)
        open_p1(exp)                       # EXPLICIT_SIZE (fixture path)
        for arm in "ABC":
            p = _beta_arm(exp, "p1", arm)
            # +31% clears A's +30% target but not B/C's +50%; observe +60% so all
            # three trigger, then book each at +50%.
            exp.observe("p1", arm, mark_price=p.entry_fill_price * 1.60, at=T0 + 5)
            exp.settle_arm("p1", arm, now=T0 + 6,
                           exit_fill=quote_exit(exp, "p1", arm,
                                                 price=p.entry_fill_price * 1.50),
                           require_quote=True)
>           assert p.status == "closed"
E           AssertionError: assert 'open' == 'closed'
E             
E             - closed
E             + open

tests/test_v2_floor_beta_notifications.py:229: 
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 
tests/test_v2_floor_beta_notifications.py:199: in bridge
    arms=parse_arms(list(arms)), decision_engine=StubDecision(),
         ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

names = ['V2_FLOOR_BETA']

    def parse_arms(names: Sequence[str]) -> tuple[ArmSpec, ...]:
        """
        Resolve a comma-separated arm list.
    
        An EMPTY list means EVERY SELECTABLE arm, and that is a DELIBERATE,
        EXPLICIT meaning rather than a default: ``parse_arms([])`` is how the
        standalone multi-arm research harness asks for all of them.
    
        "Every" is the SELECTABLE set, and it must be exactly what
        :func:`arm_names` returns -- the same vocabulary the picker and the config
        layer read. It used to be every REGISTERED arm, which silently included a
        retired one and made the two disagree about how many arms exist.
    
        It is NOT what an unselected runtime resolves to. That is
        :data:`DEFAULT_ARM`, resolved by ``RuntimeState.arms``, because "run
        everything" and "the operator chose nothing" are different situations and
        conflating them is what produced the ``L25_TP100`` selection defect.
        A RETIRED arm is refused rather than resolved: an arm that can no longer be
        selected must not be startable through the back door of a persisted list or
        a config file.
    
        ``V2_FLOOR_BETA`` (the group) is NO LONGER ACTIVE. The V2 FLOOR (BETA)
        experiment has been disabled from the active bot. Any selection that names
        the group token or a beta arm token (V2_BETA_A, V2_BETA_B, V2_BETA_C) is
        REFUSED rather than expanded, so the experiment cannot be started through
        the selector, a persisted list, or a config file.
    
        A token that names a BETA ARM (``V2_BETA_A``) previously resolved to the
        SAME group, not to that arm alone. Running one arm of a three-arm comparison
        is a different experiment and must never be mistaken for it, so a partial
        group is not a reachable outcome.
    
        The beta arm specs, enum values, and historical records are preserved: they
        remain resolvable by :func:`arm_by_name` for diagnostics and existing
        position records, but they are NOT offered for new selection and the group
        token is refused.
        """
        wanted = [n.strip() for n in names if str(n).strip()]
        if not wanted:
            # Empty means every SELECTABLE arm -- and the beta arms are NOT
            # selectable on their own, so an unselected configuration cannot
            # accidentally start the V2 FLOOR (BETA) experiment. The Amarih arms
            # are likewise group-only, so an empty selection never starts Amarih
            # either: it resolves to the L25-family production arms.
            return tuple(ARMS[a] for a in selectable_arms())
        out: list[ArmSpec] = []
        for n in wanted:
            if is_beta_group(n) or _is_beta_arm_token(n):
                # V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled. Refuse rather than expand.
>               raise ValueError(
                    f"V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled from the active bot. "
                    f"Selection of {n!r} is no longer accepted. "
                    "The experiment's code and historical records are preserved, "
                    "but no new beta positions can be opened.")
E               ValueError: V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled from the active bot. Selection of 'V2_FLOOR_BETA' is no longer accepted. The experiment's code and historical records are preserved, but no new beta positions can be opened.

        """
        Authorization is not an entry. The renderer reads the EXPERIMENT's
        book, so nothing is announced until the positions are there.
        """

_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

names = ['V2_FLOOR_BETA']

    def parse_arms(names: Sequence[str]) -> tuple[ArmSpec, ...]:
        """
        Resolve a comma-separated arm list.
    
        An EMPTY list means EVERY SELECTABLE arm, and that is a DELIBERATE,
        EXPLICIT meaning rather than a default: ``parse_arms([])`` is how the
        standalone multi-arm research harness asks for all of them.
    
        "Every" is the SELECTABLE set, and it must be exactly what
        :func:`arm_names` returns -- the same vocabulary the picker and the config
        layer read. It used to be every REGISTERED arm, which silently included a
        retired one and made the two disagree about how many arms exist.
    
        It is NOT what an unselected runtime resolves to. That is
        :data:`DEFAULT_ARM`, resolved by ``RuntimeState.arms``, because "run
        everything" and "the operator chose nothing" are different situations and
        conflating them is what produced the ``L25_TP100`` selection defect.
        A RETIRED arm is refused rather than resolved: an arm that can no longer be
        selected must not be startable through the back door of a persisted list or
        a config file.
    
        ``V2_FLOOR_BETA`` (the group) is NO LONGER ACTIVE. The V2 FLOOR (BETA)
        experiment has been disabled from the active bot. Any selection that names
        the group token or a beta arm token (V2_BETA_A, V2_BETA_B, V2_BETA_C) is
        REFUSED rather than expanded, so the experiment cannot be started through
        the selector, a persisted list, or a config file.
    
        A token that names a BETA ARM (``V2_BETA_A``) previously resolved to the
        SAME group, not to that arm alone. Running one arm of a three-arm comparison
        is a different experiment and must never be mistaken for it, so a partial
        group is not a reachable outcome.
    
        The beta arm specs, enum values, and historical records are preserved: they
        remain resolvable by :func:`arm_by_name` for diagnostics and existing
        position records, but they are NOT offered for new selection and the group
        token is refused.
        """
        wanted = [n.strip() for n in names if str(n).strip()]
        if not wanted:
            # Empty means every SELECTABLE arm -- and the beta arms are NOT
            # selectable on their own, so an unselected configuration cannot
            # accidentally start the V2 FLOOR (BETA) experiment. The Amarih arms
            # are likewise group-only, so an empty selection never starts Amarih
            # either: it resolves to the L25-family production arms.
            return tuple(ARMS[a] for a in selectable_arms())
        out: list[ArmSpec] = []
        for n in wanted:
            if is_beta_group(n) or _is_beta_arm_token(n):
                # V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled. Refuse rather than expand.
>               raise ValueError(
                    f"V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled from the active bot. "
                    f"Selection of {n!r} is no longer accepted. "
                    "The experiment's code and historical records are preserved, "
                    "but no new beta positions can be opened.")
E               ValueError: V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled from the active bot. Selection of 'V2_FLOOR_BETA' is no longer accepted. The experiment's code and historical records are preserved, but no new beta positions can be opened.

        group is not a reachable outcome.

        remain resolvable by :func:`arm_by_name` for diagnostics and existing
        position records, but they are NOT offered for new selection and the group
        token is refused.

            # selectable on their own, so an unselected configuration cannot
            # accidentally start the V2 FLOOR (BETA) experiment. The Amarih arms

            # either: it resolves to the L25-family production arms.

names = ['V2_FLOOR_BETA']

    def parse_arms(names: Sequence[str]) -> tuple[ArmSpec, ...]:
        """
        Resolve a comma-separated arm list.
    
        An EMPTY list means EVERY SELECTABLE arm, and that is a DELIBERATE,
        EXPLICIT meaning rather than a default: ``parse_arms([])`` is how the
        standalone multi-arm research harness asks for all of them.
    
        "Every" is the SELECTABLE set, and it must be exactly what
        :func:`arm_names` returns -- the same vocabulary the picker and the config
        layer read. It used to be every REGISTERED arm, which silently included a
        retired one and made the two disagree about how many arms exist.

        :data:`DEFAULT_ARM`, resolved by ``RuntimeState.arms``, because "run
        everything" and "the operator chose nothing" are different situations and
        conflating them is what produced the ``L25_TP100`` selection defect.

        selected must not be startable through the back door of a persisted list or
        a config file.

names = ['V2_FLOOR_BETA']

    def parse_arms(names: Sequence[str]) -> tuple[ArmSpec, ...]:
        """
        Resolve a comma-separated arm list.
    
        An EMPTY list means EVERY SELECTABLE arm, and that is a DELIBERATE,
        EXPLICIT meaning rather than a default: ``parse_arms([])`` is how the
        standalone multi-arm research harness asks for all of them.
    
        "Every" is the SELECTABLE set, and it must be exactly what
        :func:`arm_names` returns -- the same vocabulary the picker and the config
        layer read. It used to be every REGISTERED arm, which silently included a
        retired one and made the two disagree about how many arms exist.
    
        It is NOT what an unselected runtime resolves to. That is
        :data:`DEFAULT_ARM`, resolved by ``RuntimeState.arms``, because "run
        everything" and "the operator chose nothing" are different situations and
        conflating them is what produced the ``L25_TP100`` selection defect.
        A RETIRED arm is refused rather than resolved: an arm that can no longer be
        selected must not be startable through the back door of a persisted list or
        a config file.
    
        ``V2_FLOOR_BETA`` (the group) is NO LONGER ACTIVE. The V2 FLOOR (BETA)
        experiment has been disabled from the active bot. Any selection that names
        the group token or a beta arm token (V2_BETA_A, V2_BETA_B, V2_BETA_C) is
        REFUSED rather than expanded, so the experiment cannot be started through
        the selector, a persisted list, or a config file.
    
        A token that names a BETA ARM (``V2_BETA_A``) previously resolved to the
        SAME group, not to that arm alone. Running one arm of a three-arm comparison
        is a different experiment and must never be mistaken for it, so a partial
        group is not a reachable outcome.
    
        The beta arm specs, enum values, and historical records are preserved: they
        remain resolvable by :func:`arm_by_name` for diagnostics and existing
        position records, but they are NOT offered for new selection and the group
        token is refused.
        """
        wanted = [n.strip() for n in names if str(n).strip()]
        if not wanted:
            # Empty means every SELECTABLE arm -- and the beta arms are NOT
            # selectable on their own, so an unselected configuration cannot
            # accidentally start the V2 FLOOR (BETA) experiment. The Amarih arms
            # are likewise group-only, so an empty selection never starts Amarih
            # either: it resolves to the L25-family production arms.
            return tuple(ARMS[a] for a in selectable_arms())
        out: list[ArmSpec] = []
        for n in wanted:
            if is_beta_group(n) or _is_beta_arm_token(n):
                # V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled. Refuse rather than expand.
>               raise ValueError(
                    f"V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled from the active bot. "
                    f"Selection of {n!r} is no longer accepted. "
                    "The experiment's code and historical records are preserved, "
                    "but no new beta positions can be opened.")
E               ValueError: V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled from the active bot. Selection of 'V2_FLOOR_BETA' is no longer accepted. The experiment's code and historical records are preserved, but no new beta positions can be opened.

_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

names = ['V2_FLOOR_BETA']

    def parse_arms(names: Sequence[str]) -> tuple[ArmSpec, ...]:
        """
        Resolve a comma-separated arm list.
    
        An EMPTY list means EVERY SELECTABLE arm, and that is a DELIBERATE,
        EXPLICIT meaning rather than a default: ``parse_arms([])`` is how the
        standalone multi-arm research harness asks for all of them.
    
        "Every" is the SELECTABLE set, and it must be exactly what
        :func:`arm_names` returns -- the same vocabulary the picker and the config
        layer read. It used to be every REGISTERED arm, which silently included a
        retired one and made the two disagree about how many arms exist.
    
        It is NOT what an unselected runtime resolves to. That is
        :data:`DEFAULT_ARM`, resolved by ``RuntimeState.arms``, because "run
        everything" and "the operator chose nothing" are different situations and
        conflating them is what produced the ``L25_TP100`` selection defect.
        A RETIRED arm is refused rather than resolved: an arm that can no longer be
        selected must not be startable through the back door of a persisted list or
        a config file.
    
        ``V2_FLOOR_BETA`` (the group) is NO LONGER ACTIVE. The V2 FLOOR (BETA)
        experiment has been disabled from the active bot. Any selection that names
        the group token or a beta arm token (V2_BETA_A, V2_BETA_B, V2_BETA_C) is
        REFUSED rather than expanded, so the experiment cannot be started through
        the selector, a persisted list, or a config file.
    
        A token that names a BETA ARM (``V2_BETA_A``) previously resolved to the
        SAME group, not to that arm alone. Running one arm of a three-arm comparison
        is a different experiment and must never be mistaken for it, so a partial
        group is not a reachable outcome.
    
        The beta arm specs, enum values, and historical records are preserved: they
        remain resolvable by :func:`arm_by_name` for diagnostics and existing
        position records, but they are NOT offered for new selection and the group
        token is refused.
        """
        wanted = [n.strip() for n in names if str(n).strip()]
        if not wanted:
            # Empty means every SELECTABLE arm -- and the beta arms are NOT
            # selectable on their own, so an unselected configuration cannot
            # accidentally start the V2 FLOOR (BETA) experiment. The Amarih arms
            # are likewise group-only, so an empty selection never starts Amarih
            # either: it resolves to the L25-family production arms.
            return tuple(ARMS[a] for a in selectable_arms())
        out: list[ArmSpec] = []
        for n in wanted:
            if is_beta_group(n) or _is_beta_arm_token(n):
                # V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled. Refuse rather than expand.
>               raise ValueError(
                    f"V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled from the active bot. "
                    f"Selection of {n!r} is no longer accepted. "
                    "The experiment's code and historical records are preserved, "
                    "but no new beta positions can be opened.")
E               ValueError: V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled from the active bot. Selection of 'V2_FLOOR_BETA' is no longer accepted. The experiment's code and historical records are preserved, but no new beta positions can be opened.

tests/test_v2_floor_beta_notifications.py:707: in _beta_bridge
    arms=parse_arms(["V2_FLOOR_BETA"]), decision_engine=StubDecision(),
         ^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^^
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

names = ['V2_FLOOR_BETA']

    def parse_arms(names: Sequence[str]) -> tuple[ArmSpec, ...]:
        """
        Resolve a comma-separated arm list.
    
        An EMPTY list means EVERY SELECTABLE arm, and that is a DELIBERATE,
        EXPLICIT meaning rather than a default: ``parse_arms([])`` is how the
        standalone multi-arm research harness asks for all of them.
    
        "Every" is the SELECTABLE set, and it must be exactly what
        :func:`arm_names` returns -- the same vocabulary the picker and the config
        layer read. It used to be every REGISTERED arm, which silently included a
        retired one and made the two disagree about how many arms exist.
    
        It is NOT what an unselected runtime resolves to. That is
        :data:`DEFAULT_ARM`, resolved by ``RuntimeState.arms``, because "run
        everything" and "the operator chose nothing" are different situations and
        conflating them is what produced the ``L25_TP100`` selection defect.
        A RETIRED arm is refused rather than resolved: an arm that can no longer be
        selected must not be startable through the back door of a persisted list or
        a config file.
    
        ``V2_FLOOR_BETA`` (the group) is NO LONGER ACTIVE. The V2 FLOOR (BETA)
        experiment has been disabled from the active bot. Any selection that names
        the group token or a beta arm token (V2_BETA_A, V2_BETA_B, V2_BETA_C) is
        REFUSED rather than expanded, so the experiment cannot be started through
        the selector, a persisted list, or a config file.
    
        A token that names a BETA ARM (``V2_BETA_A``) previously resolved to the
        SAME group, not to that arm alone. Running one arm of a three-arm comparison
        is a different experiment and must never be mistaken for it, so a partial
        group is not a reachable outcome.
    
        The beta arm specs, enum values, and historical records are preserved: they
        remain resolvable by :func:`arm_by_name` for diagnostics and existing
        position records, but they are NOT offered for new selection and the group
        token is refused.
        """
        wanted = [n.strip() for n in names if str(n).strip()]
        if not wanted:
            # Empty means every SELECTABLE arm -- and the beta arms are NOT
            # selectable on their own, so an unselected configuration cannot
            # accidentally start the V2 FLOOR (BETA) experiment. The Amarih arms
            # are likewise group-only, so an empty selection never starts Amarih
            # either: it resolves to the L25-family production arms.
            return tuple(ARMS[a] for a in selectable_arms())
        out: list[ArmSpec] = []
        for n in wanted:
            if is_beta_group(n) or _is_beta_arm_token(n):
                # V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled. Refuse rather than expand.
>               raise ValueError(
                    f"V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled from the active bot. "
                    f"Selection of {n!r} is no longer accepted. "
                    "The experiment's code and historical records are preserved, "
                    "but no new beta positions can be opened.")
E               ValueError: V2 FLOOR (BETA) is disabled from the active bot. Selection of 'V2_FLOOR_BETA' is no longer accepted. The experiment's code and historical records are preserved, but no new beta positions can be opened.

    def test_a_beta_only_monitor_tick_actually_delivers_the_receipt(
            self, tmp_path):
        """The flush must not be gated on a regular ``TradeRecord``.
    
        A beta arm is settled by ``BetaExperiment``, so a tick that settles one
        produces NO entry in ``trades``. Gating the flush on ``trades`` meant
        that under a BETA-ONLY selection -- where no regular arm exists at all --
        a beta CLOSE and a beta EXIT BLOCKED were queued and NEVER delivered.
        """

owner = 'wUEUbVmz2EfcLfchHmucTKhRpZgDpsBvo3FttZ4Wbt6'
accounts = [('11111111111111111111111111111111', 'TokenzQdBNbLqP5VEhdkAS6EPFLC1PHnBqCXEpPxuEb')]

    def close_account_instructions(owner: str, accounts) -> list:
        """
        Build one SPL ``CloseAccount`` instruction per ``(token_account, program)``.
    
        PURE AND OFFLINE: nothing is signed and nothing is sent. The rent that was
        bonded to the account is returned to ``owner``, which must be the account's
        own owner AND the signer -- the token program enforces both. The program id
        is the account's OWN program, so a Token-2022 account is closed by
        Token-2022 and a classic account by the classic program; using the wrong one
        would fail on chain.
    
        Lives in the execution layer because it is the only layer permitted to
        import a chain library.
        """
>       from solders.instruction import AccountMeta, Instruction
E       ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'solders'

    def test_flattened_gateway_response_shape_is_understood():
        """Some gateways return {"value": [...]}. A parser assuming one shape would
        report 'no wSOL account' for a wallet that has one and block a good trade."""
        class Flat(_Rpc):
            async def _rpc(self, method, params):
                self.calls.append((method, params))
                return {"value": [_wsol_account()]}

tests/test_wsol_size.py:251: 
_ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ _ 

    """Deterministic Solana transaction fixtures for the version-aware tests.
    
    Everything here is OFFLINE and built with ``solders``: no network, no RPC, no
    funded key. Ephemeral in-memory keypairs only.
    
    Why this module exists
    ----------------------
    A serialized transaction is now judged by its DESERIALIZED version, so a test
    that hands the executor an arbitrary string like ``"AQAB"`` is no longer
    modelling anything real -- the size gate must refuse it, correctly. These
    helpers build transactions that are genuinely valid, so the tests exercise the
    production paths rather than tripping over a fixture that could never occur.
    
    The builders are parameterised by VERSION and by TARGET SIZE, because the whole
    point of the V1 work is that a transaction's verdict depends on which of those
    it is:
    
        legacy  -> 1232 byte ceiling
        v0      -> 1232 byte ceiling
        v1      -> 4096 byte ceiling
    """
    
    from __future__ import annotations
    
    import base64
    from typing import Optional
    
>   from solders.hash import Hash
E   ModuleNotFoundError: No module named 'solders'
